43:     voter = db.query(Voter).filter(Voter.email == user.email).first()
44: 
45:     if not voter:
46:         raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid email")
47: 
48:     if not verify_password(user.password, voter.password):
49:         raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid password")

51:     token = create_access_token(data={"sub": voter.email})
52: 
53:     return {
54:         "access_token": token,

110:         return

119:         'ALTER TABLE votes ADD COLUMN timestamp DATETIME',
120:         'ALTER TABLE audit_logs ADD COLUMN timestamp DATETIME',
121:     ]
122: 
123:     for statement in statements:
124:         try:
125:             conn.exec_driver_sql(statement)
126:         except Exception as exc:
127:             message = str(exc).lower()
128:             if "duplicate column name" not in message and "already exists" not in message:

34: 
35:     # Step 2: Migrate Candidates
36:     async with SqliteSessionLocal() as sqlite_session, PgSessionLocal() as pg_session:
37:         # We process each model
38:         # Candidates first because Votes have a ForeignKey relation to Candidates
39:         print("\nMigrating Candidates...")
40:         candidate_res = await sqlite_session.execute(select(Candidate))
41:         candidates = candidate_res.scalars().all()
42:         print(f"Found {len(candidates)} candidates in SQLite.")
43:         
44:         # Check if candidates already exist in Postgres to prevent duplicates
45:         pg_candidate_res = await pg_session.execute(select(Candidate))
46:         pg_candidates = pg_candidate_res.scalars().all()
47:         if len(pg_candidates) > 0:
48:             print("Candidates already exist in PostgreSQL. Skipping Candidates migration.")
49:         else:
50:             for c in candidates:
51:                 new_c = Candidate(
52:                     id=c.id,
53:                     name=c.name,
54:                     party=c.party,
55:                     symbol=c.symbol,
56:                     district=c.district,
57:                     constituency=c.constituency,
58:                     votes=c.votes,
59:                     created_at=c.created_at
60:                 )
61:                 pg_session.add(new_c)
62:             await pg_session.commit()
63:             print("Candidates migrated successfully.")
64: 
65:         # Step 3: Migrate Voters
66:         print("\nMigrating Voters...")
67:         voter_res = await sqlite_session.execute(select(Voter))
68:         voters = voter_res.scalars().all()
69:         print(f"Found {len(voters)} voters in SQLite.")
70:         
71:         # Check if voters already exist in Postgres
72:         pg_voter_res = await pg_session.execute(select(Voter))
73:         pg_voters = pg_voter_res.scalars().all()
74:         existing_voters_ids = {v.voter_id for v in pg_voters}
75:         
76:         migrated_voters_count = 0
77:         for v in voters:
78:             if v.voter_id not in existing_voters_ids:
79:                 new_v = Voter(
80:                     id=v.id,
81:                     voter_id=v.voter_id,
82:                     full_name=v.full_name,
83:                     email=v.email,
84:                     password=v.password,
85:                     cnic=v.cnic,
86:                     district=v.district,
87:                     phone=v.phone,
88:                     constituency=v.constituency,
89:                     has_voted=v.has_voted,
90:                     is_verified=v.is_verified,
91:                     face_embedding=v.face_embedding,
92:                     is_pending=v.is_pending,
93:                     pending_reason=v.pending_reason,
94:                     registration_hash=v.registration_hash,
95:                     created_at=v.created_at
96:                 )
97:                 pg_session.add(new_v)
98:                 migrated_voters_count += 1
99:         
100:         if migrated_voters_count > 0:
101:             await pg_session.commit()
102:             print(f"Migrated {migrated_voters_count} voters successfully.")
103:         else:
104:             print("No new voters to migrate.")
105: 
106:         # Step 4: Migrate Votes
107:         print("\nMigrating Votes...")
108:         vote_res = await sqlite_session.execute(select(Vote))
109:         votes = vote_res.scalars().all()
110:         print(f"Found {len(votes)} votes in SQLite.")
111:         
112:         pg_vote_res = await pg_session.execute(select(Vote))
113:         pg_votes = pg_vote_res.scalars().all()
114:         existing_vote_codes = {vt.receipt_code for vt in pg_votes}
115:         
116:         migrated_votes_count = 0
117:         for vt in votes:
118:             if vt.receipt_code not in existing_vote_codes:
119:                 new_vt = Vote(
120:                     id=vt.id,
121:                     voter_id=vt.voter_id,
122:                     candidate_id=vt.candidate_id,
123:                     receipt_code=vt.receipt_code,
124:                     vote_hash=vt.vote_hash,
125:                     blockchain_hash=vt.blockchain_hash,
126:                     timestamp=vt.timestamp,
127:                     created_at=vt.created_at
128:                 )
129:                 pg_session.add(new_vt)
130:                 migrated_votes_count += 1
131:                 
132:         if migrated_votes_count > 0:
133:             await pg_session.commit()
134:             print(f"Migrated {migrated_votes_count} votes successfully.")
135:         else:
136:             print("No new votes to migrate.")
137: 
138:         # Step 5: Migrate Audit Logs
139:         print("\nMigrating Audit Logs...")
140:         audit_res = await sqlite_session.execute(select(AuditLog))
141:         audits = audit_res.scalars().all()
142:         print(f"Found {len(audits)} audit logs in SQLite.")
143:         
144:         # Check count of existing pg audit logs to avoid duplicate migration
145:         pg_audit_count_res = await pg_session.execute(select(AuditLog))
146:         pg_audits = pg_audit_count_res.scalars().all()
147:         
148:         if len(pg_audits) > 0:
149:             print("Audit logs already exist in PostgreSQL. Skipping Audit Logs migration to avoid duplicates.")
150:         else:
151:             for a in audits:
152:                 new_a = AuditLog(
153:                     id=a.id,
154:                     action=a.action,
155:                     details=a.details,
156:                     timestamp=a.timestamp,
157:                     severity=a.severity,
158:                     created_at=a.created_at
159:                 )
160:                 pg_session.add(new_a)
161:             if len(audits) > 0:
162:                 await pg_session.commit()
163:                 print(f"Migrated {len(audits)} audit logs successfully.")
164:             else:
165:                 print("No audit logs to migrate.")
166: 
167:     print("\nDatabase migration completed successfully!")
168: 
169: if __name__ == "__main__":
170:     asyncio.run(migrate())

24: * `GET /admin/dashboard/summary`: Computes total voters, votes cast, turnout percentage, total candidates, total districts, and resolved/unresolved security incidents.
25: * `GET /admin/dashboard/voting-trend`: Pulls vote timestamps and voter registration dates for the last 30 days. Uses dialect-aware query generation supporting both **PostgreSQL** (`date_trunc`) and **SQLite** (`strftime` / `date`) for seamless local development and production.

8: from sqlalchemy import func as sqlfunc
9: from app.database import AsyncSessionLocal
10: from app.models import Voter, Vote, District
11: 
12: async def check():
13:     async with AsyncSessionLocal() as db:
14:         # 1. Compare Stats
15:         total_voters_stats = await db.scalar(select(sqlfunc.count(Voter.id)))
16:         # stats endpoint counts voters where has_voted == True
17:         votes_cast_stats = await db.scalar(select(sqlfunc.count(Voter.id)).where(Voter.has_voted == True))
18:         
19:         # summary endpoint counts from votes table
20:         total_voters_summary = await db.scalar(select(sqlfunc.count(Voter.id)))
21:         total_votes_cast_summary = await db.scalar(select(sqlfunc.count(Vote.id)))
22:         
23:         print("=== Stat Comparison ===")
24:         print(f"Stats Endpoint: total_voters={total_voters_stats}, votes_cast={votes_cast_stats}")
25:         print(f"Summary Endpoint: total_voters={total_voters_summary}, total_votes_cast={total_votes_cast_summary}")
26:         
27:         # 2. Distinct district values
28:         voter_districts_res = await db.execute(select(Voter.district).distinct())
29:         voter_districts = [r[0] for r in voter_districts_res.all() if r[0]]
30:         
31:         district_names_res = await db.execute(select(District.district_name).distinct())
32:         district_names = [r[0] for r in district_names_res.all() if r[0]]
33:         
34:         print("\n=== District Name Comparison ===")
35:         print(f"Distinct values in voters.district: {voter_districts}")
36:         print(f"Distinct values in districts.district_name: {district_names}")
37:         
38:         # Match check
39:         matches = [d for d in voter_districts if d in district_names]
40:         mismatches = [d for d in voter_districts if d not in district_names]
41:         print(f"Matches: {matches}")
42:         print(f"Mismatches: {mismatches}")
43: 
44: if __name__ == "__main__":
45:     asyncio.run(check())
46: 
The above content shows the entire, complete file contents of the requested file.

1: from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException
2: from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
3: 
4: from app.database import get_db
5: from app.models import Voter
6: from app.schemas import VoterCreate, LoginSchema
7: 
8: from app.utils.security import hash_password, verify_password
9: from app.utils.jwt_handler import create_access_token
10: 
11: router = APIRouter()
12: 
13: 
14: # REGISTER API
15: @router.post("/register")
16: def register(voter: VoterCreate, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
17: 
18:     existing = db.query(Voter).filter(Voter.email == voter.email).first()
19: 
20:     if existing:
21:         raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Email already exists")
22: 
23:     hashed = hash_password(voter.password)
24: 
25:     new_voter = Voter(
26:         full_name=voter.full_name,
27:         email=voter.email,
28:         password=hashed,
29:         cnic=voter.cnic,
30:         district=voter.district
31:     )
32: 
33:     db.add(new_voter)
34:     db.commit()
35: 
36:     return {"message": "User registered successfully"}
37: 
38: 
39: # LOGIN API
40: @router.post("/login")
41: def login(user: LoginSchema, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
42: 
43:     voter = db.query(Voter).filter(Voter.email == user.email).first()
44: 
45:     if not voter:
46:         raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid email")
47: 
48:     if not verify_password(user.password, voter.password):
49:         raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid password")
50: 
51:     token = create_access_token(data={"sub": voter.email})
52: 
53:     return {
54:         "access_token": token,
55:         "token_type": "bearer"
56:     }

116: def calculate_vote_hash(voter_id: int, candidate_id: int, receipt_code: str) -> str:
117:     return hashlib.sha256(f"{voter_id}{candidate_id}{receipt_code}".encode()).hexdigest()
118: 
119: 
120: def ensure_phase_one_schema(conn):
121: 
122:     if conn.dialect.name != "sqlite":
123:         return
124: 
125:     statements = [
126:         'ALTER TABLE voters ADD COLUMN email VARCHAR',
127:         'ALTER TABLE voters ADD COLUMN password VARCHAR',
128:         'ALTER TABLE voters ADD COLUMN district VARCHAR',
129:         'ALTER TABLE voters ADD COLUMN registration_hash VARCHAR',
130:         'ALTER TABLE candidates ADD COLUMN district VARCHAR',
131:         'ALTER TABLE votes ADD COLUMN vote_hash VARCHAR',
132:         'ALTER TABLE votes ADD COLUMN timestamp DATETIME',
133:         'ALTER TABLE audit_logs ADD COLUMN timestamp DATETIME',
134:     ]
135: 
136:     for statement in statements:
137:         try:
138:             conn.exec_driver_sql(statement)
139:         except Exception as exc:
140:             message = str(exc).lower()
141:             if "duplicate column name" not in message and "already exists" not in message:
142:                 raise
143: 
144: 
145: def create_admin_token():
146: 
147:     expire = datetime.utcnow() + timedelta(hours=ADMIN_TOKEN_EXPIRES_HOURS)
148: 
149:     payload = {
150:         "sub": ADMIN_USERNAME,
151:         "role": "admin",
152:         "exp": expire
153:     }
154: 
155:     return jwt.encode(
156:         payload,
157:         ADMIN_JWT_SECRET,
158:         algorithm=ADMIN_TOKEN_ALGORITHM
159:     )
160: 
161: 
162: def require_admin(
163: 
164:     credentials: HTTPAuthorizationCredentials = Depends(security)
165: 
166: ):
167: 
168:     if not ADMIN_USERNAME or not ADMIN_PASSWORD:
169: 
170:         raise HTTPException(
171:             status_code=status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
172:             detail="Admin credentials not configured"
173:         )
174: 
175:     if not credentials:
176: 
177:         raise HTTPException(
178:             status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
179:             detail="Not authenticated"
180:         )
181: 
182:     try:
183: 
184:         payload = jwt.decode(
185:             credentials.credentials,
186:             ADMIN_JWT_SECRET,
187:             algorithms=[ADMIN_TOKEN_ALGORITHM]
188:         )
189: 
190:     except JWTError:
191: 
192:         raise HTTPException(
193:             status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
194:             detail="Invalid token"
195:         )
196: 
197:     if payload.get("sub") != ADMIN_USERNAME or payload.get("role") != "admin":
198: 
199:         raise HTTPException(
200:             status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
201:             detail="Not authorized"
202:         )
203: 
204:     return payload
205: 
206: 
207: def get_current_voter(credentials: HTTPAuthorizationCredentials = Depends(security)):
208: 
209:     if not credentials:
210: 
211:         raise HTTPException(
212:             status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
213:             detail="Not authenticated"
214:         )
215: 
216:     try:
217: 
218:         payload = jwt.decode(
219:             credentials.credentials,
220:             VOTER_JWT_SECRET,
221:             algorithms=[VOTER_JWT_ALGORITHM]
222:         )
223: 
224:     except JWTError:
225: 
226:         raise HTTPException(
227:             status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
228:             detail="Invalid token"
229:         )
230: 
231:     if payload.get("role") != "voter":
232: 
233:         raise HTTPException(
234:             status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
235:             detail="Not authorized"
236:         )
237: 
238:     return payload

The following code has been modified to include a line number before every line, in the format: <line_number>: <original_line>. Please note that any changes targeting the original code should remove the line number, colon, and leading space.
1: """
2: face_service.py
3: 
4: Face embedding extraction and matching using MediaPipe Face Mesh + OpenCV.
5: 
6: Extracts a 128-point normalized landmark vector as a face "embedding".
7: Compares embeddings using cosine similarity.
8: 
9: No heavy ML model download needed — uses MediaPipe which is already installed.
10: """
11: 
12: import cv2
13: import numpy as np
14: import base64
15: import json
16: from io import BytesIO
17: from PIL import Image
18: import mediapipe as mp
19: 
20: # ── MediaPipe setup ───────────────────────────────────────────
21: _mp_face_mesh = mp.solutions.face_mesh
22: _face_mesh = _mp_face_mesh.FaceMesh(
23:     static_image_mode=True,
24:     max_num_faces=1,
25:     refine_landmarks=True,
26:     min_detection_confidence=0.5,
27: )
28: 
29: # Similarity threshold — tune if needed
30: MATCH_THRESHOLD = 0.999
31: 
32: 
33: def _decode_image(image_b64: str) -> np.ndarray:
34:     """Decode a base64 image string to an OpenCV BGR array."""
35:     # Strip data URL prefix if present
36:     if "," in image_b64:
37:         image_b64 = image_b64.split(",", 1)[1]
38: 
39:     img_bytes = base64.b64decode(image_b64)
40:     img_array = np.frombuffer(img_bytes, dtype=np.uint8)
41:     img = cv2.imdecode(img_array, cv2.IMREAD_COLOR)
42:     return img
43: 
44: 
45: def _extract_embedding(image_b64: str) -> list | None:
46:     """
47:     Extract a normalized face landmark embedding from a base64 image.
48:     Returns a flat list of floats, or None if no face detected.
49:     """
50:     img = _decode_image(image_b64)
51:     if img is None:
52:         return None
53: 
54:     # Convert BGR → RGB for MediaPipe
55:     img_rgb = cv2.cvtColor(img, cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)
56:     results = _face_mesh.process(img_rgb)
57: 
58:     if not results.multi_face_landmarks:
59:         return None
60: 
61:     landmarks = results.multi_face_landmarks[0].landmark
62: 
63:     # Use 128 key landmarks (covers full face geometry)
64:     KEY_POINTS = [
65:         # Jawline
66:         10, 338, 297, 332, 284, 251, 389, 356, 454, 323, 361, 288,
67:         397, 365, 379, 378, 400, 377, 152, 148, 176, 149, 150, 136,
68:         172, 58, 132, 93, 234, 127, 162, 21, 54, 103, 67, 109,
69:         # Eyes
70:         33, 7, 163, 144, 145, 153, 154, 155, 133, 173, 157, 158,
71:         159, 160, 161, 246, 362, 382, 381, 380, 374, 373, 390, 249,
72:         263, 466, 388, 387, 386, 385, 384, 398,
73:         # Nose
74:         1, 2, 5, 4, 19, 94, 2, 164, 0, 11, 12, 13, 14, 15, 16, 17,
75:         # Mouth
76:         61, 185, 40, 39, 37, 0, 267, 269, 270, 409, 291, 375, 321,
77:         405, 314, 17, 84, 181, 91, 146, 76, 77, 90, 180, 85, 16,
78:         # Cheeks and forehead
79:         116, 123, 147, 213, 192, 214, 210, 211, 32, 208, 199, 428,
80:         262, 431, 432, 434, 430, 394
81:     ]
82: 
83:     # Deduplicate while preserving order
84:     seen = set()
85:     unique_points = []
86:     for p in KEY_POINTS:
87:         if p not in seen and p < len(landmarks):
88:             seen.add(p)
89:             unique_points.append(p)
90: 
91:     coords = []
92:     for idx in unique_points:
93:         lm = landmarks[idx]
94:         coords.extend([lm.x, lm.y, lm.z])
95: 
96:     embedding = np.array(coords, dtype=np.float32)
97: 
98:     # Normalize to unit vector (cosine similarity friendly)
99:     norm = np.linalg.norm(embedding)
100:     if norm == 0:
101:         return None
102: 
103:     embedding = embedding / norm
104:     return embedding.tolist()
105: 
106: 
107: def _cosine_similarity(a: list, b: list) -> float:
108:     """Cosine similarity between two embedding vectors."""
109:     va = np.array(a, dtype=np.float32)
110:     vb = np.array(b, dtype=np.float32)
111:     dot = np.dot(va, vb)
112:     norm = np.linalg.norm(va) * np.linalg.norm(vb)
113:     if norm == 0:
114:         return 0.0
115:     return float(dot / norm)
116: 
117: 
118: # ── Public API ────────────────────────────────────────────────
119: 
120: def extract_embedding(image_b64: str) -> dict:
121:     """
122:     Extract face embedding from base64 image.
123:     Returns { success, embedding, message }
124:     """
125:     try:
126:         embedding = _extract_embedding(image_b64)
127:         if embedding is None:
128:             return {
129:                 "success": False,
130:                 "embedding": None,
131:                 "message": "No face detected in image"
132:             }
133:         return {
134:             "success": True,
135:             "embedding": embedding,
136:             "message": "Face embedding extracted"
137:         }
138:     except Exception as e:
139:         print(f"[FaceService] extract_embedding error: {e}")
140:         return {
141:             "success": False,
142:             "embedding": None,
143:             "message": "Failed to process image"
144:         }
145: 
146: 
147: def match_faces(stored_embedding_json: str, live_image_b64: str) -> dict:
148:     """
149:     Compare stored embedding (JSON string) against a live image.
150:     Returns { match, similarity, message }
151:     """
152:     try:
153:         stored = json.loads(stored_embedding_json)
154:         live_result = _extract_embedding(live_image_b64)
155: 
156:         if live_result is None:
157:             return {
158:                 "match": False,
159:                 "similarity": 0.0,
160:                 "message": "No face detected in live image"
161:             }
162: 
163:         similarity = _cosine_similarity(stored, live_result)
164:         matched = similarity >= MATCH_THRESHOLD
165: 
166:         return {
167:             "match": matched,
168:             "similarity": round(similarity, 4),
169:             "message": "Face matched" if matched else "Face did not match"
170:         }
171: 
172:     except Exception as e:
173:         print(f"[FaceService] match_faces error: {e}")
174:         return {
175:             "match": False,
176:             "similarity": 0.0,
177:             "message": "Face comparison failed"
178:         }
179:

1: # Goal Description
2: 
3: The user has requested to remove the "Pending Reviews" feature while using a safe approach that does not damage the existing project. This feature currently flags voters for manual review when their face scan fails, creating a backlog that an admin must manually approve or deny. We will completely remove this logic.
4: 
5: ## User Review Required
6: 
7: > [!WARNING]
8: > Removing this feature changes the fallback behavior for failed facial verification.
9: > Currently, a failed face match marks the voter as "Pending" and prevents them from voting until an admin reviews it.
10: > With this change, a failed face match will **immediately deny** the voter, requiring them to try again or contact support directly outside the app.
11: 
12: > [!IMPORTANT]
13: > The database columns (`is_pending`, `pending_reason`) will be removed from the SQLAlchemy model. SQLAlchemy safely ignores extra columns in the database, so we do not need to run a database migration or drop the columns manually (preventing any accidental data loss).
14:

6: 
7: ## 1. Timezone Bugfix
8: 
9: - **Issue**: A `TypeError: can't compare offset-naive and offset-aware datetimes` caused the backend to crash inside `is_recent` checks (under audit log queries) due to a timezone mismatch.
10: - **Resolution**: Updated `is_recent` in [main.py](file:///C:/Users/Admin/Desktop/hvverify/hc-verify-main/backend/main.py) to strip timezone metadata before performing comparison:
11:   ```python
12:   def is_recent(entry: AuditLog) -> bool:
13:       t = log_time(entry)
14:       if not t:
15:           return False
16:       return t.replace(tzinfo=None) >= window_start.replace(tzinfo=None)
17:   ```
18: 
19: ---
20: 
21: ## 2. Removal of "Pending Reviews" Feature
22: 
23: To simplify administration and avoid voter reviews backlog, the pending review state was removed:
24: - **Voter Model**: Removed `is_pending` and `pending_reason` fields from the SQLAlchemy `Voter` schema inside [models.py](file:///C:/Users/Admin/Desktop/hvverify/hc-verify-main/backend/app/models.py).
25: - **Face Verification**: Modified `/verify-face` to return an immediate failure response rather than updating the database to flag the voter as pending.
26: - **Backend Endpoints**: Deleted the `GET /admin/pending-voters`, `POST /admin/resolve-pending/{voter_id}`, and `POST /admin/flag-voter/{voter_id}` endpoints from [main.py](file:///C:/Users/Admin/Desktop/hvverify/hc-verify-main/backend/main.py).
27: - **Frontend Cleanup**: Removed the "Pending Reviews" tab and card, deleted the "Pending Investigation" panel code block, and cleaned up its states/helpers inside [AdminPage.jsx](file:///C:/Users/Admin/Desktop/hvverify/hc-verify-main/frontend/src/pages/AdminPage.jsx).
28:

262:   // Helper function to generate SHA256 locally using Web Cryptography API
263:   const sha256 = async (message) => {
264:     const msgBuffer = new TextEncoder().encode(message);
265:     const hashBuffer = await crypto.subtle.digest("SHA-256", msgBuffer);
266:     const hashArray = Array.from(new Uint8Array(hashBuffer));
267:     return hashArray.map(b => b.toString(16).padStart(2, "0")).join("");
268:   };

14: @router.get("/", response_model=list[UserResponse])
15: async def get_all_users(db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
16:     """Get all admin users."""
17:     result = await db.execute(select(User).order_by(User.created_at.desc()))
18:     return result.scalars().all()
19: 
20: 
21: @router.get("/count")
22: async def get_user_count(db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
23:     """Get total user count for dashboard stats."""
24:     result = await db.execute(select(func.count(User.user_id)))
25:     count = result.scalar()
26:     return {"total_users": count}
27: 
28: 
29: @router.get("/{user_id}", response_model=UserResponse)
30: async def get_user(user_id: uuid.UUID, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
31:     """Get a single user by UUID."""
32:     result = await db.execute(select(User).where(User.user_id == user_id))
33:     user = result.scalars().first()
34:     if not user:
35:         raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="User not found")
36:     return user
37: 
38: 
39: @router.post("/", response_model=UserResponse, status_code=201)
40: async def create_user(payload: UserCreate, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
41:     """Create a new admin user."""
42:     # Check if email already exists
43:     existing = await db.execute(select(User).where(User.email == payload.email))
44:     if existing.scalars().first():
45:         raise HTTPException(
46:             status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
47:             detail="A user with this email already exists."
48:         )
49: 
50:     new_user = User(
51:         user_id=uuid.uuid4(),
52:         full_name=payload.full_name,
53:         email=payload.email,
54:         role=payload.role,
55:     )
56:     db.add(new_user)
57:     await db.commit()
58:     await db.refresh(new_user)
59:     return new_user
60: 
61: 
62: @router.put("/{user_id}", response_model=UserResponse)
63: async def update_user(
64:     user_id: uuid.UUID,
65:     payload: UserUpdate,
66:     db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)
67: ):
68:     """Update an existing user."""
69:     result = await db.execute(select(User).where(User.user_id == user_id))
70:     user = result.scalars().first()
71:     if not user:
72:         raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="User not found")
73: 
74:     if payload.full_name is not None:
75:         user.full_name = payload.full_name
76:     if payload.email is not None:
77:         # Check email uniqueness excluding current user
78:         existing = await db.execute(
79:             select(User).where(User.email == payload.email, User.user_id != user_id)
80:         )
81:         if existing.scalars().first():
82:             raise HTTPException(
83:                 status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
84:                 detail="Email already in use by another user."
85:             )
86:         user.email = payload.email
87:     if payload.role is not None:
88:         user.role = payload.role
89: 
90:     await db.commit()
91:     await db.refresh(user)
92:     return user
93: 
94: 
95: @router.delete("/{user_id}", status_code=204)
96: async def delete_user(user_id: uuid.UUID, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
97:     """Delete a user."""
98:     result = await db.execute(select(User).where(User.user_id == user_id))
99:     user = result.scalars().first()
100:     if not user:
101:         raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="User not found")
102:     await db.delete(user)
103:     await db.commit()

15: class ElectionCreate(BaseModel):
16:     title: str
17:     date: datetime
18:     status: str = "Upcoming"
19:

21: class ElectionResponse(BaseModel):
22:     election_id: uuid.UUID
23:     title: str

25:     status: str

32: @router.get("/", response_model=list[ElectionResponse])
33: async def get_all_elections(db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
34:     """Return all elections from the database."""
35:     result = await db.execute(select(Election).order_by(Election.created_at.desc()))
36:     return result.scalars().all()
37: 
38: 
39: @router.get("/{election_id}", response_model=ElectionResponse)
40: async def get_election(election_id: uuid.UUID, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
41:     """Return a single election by UUID."""
42:     result = await db.execute(select(Election).where(Election.election_id == election_id))
43:     election = result.scalars().first()
44:     if not election:
45:         raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Election not found")
46:     return election
47: 
48: 
49: @router.post("/", response_model=ElectionResponse, status_code=201)
50: async def create_election(payload: ElectionCreate, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
51:     """Create a new election."""
52:     new_election = Election(
53:         election_id=uuid.uuid4(),
54:         title=payload.title,
55:         date=payload.date,
56:         status=payload.status,
57:     )
58:     db.add(new_election)
59:     await db.commit()
60:     await db.refresh(new_election)
61:     return new_election
62: 
63: 
64: @router.delete("/{election_id}", status_code=204)
65: async def delete_election(election_id: uuid.UUID, db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db)):
66:     """Delete an election by UUID."""
67:     result = await db.execute(select(Election).where(Election.election_id == election_id))
68:     election = result.scalars().first()
69:     if not election:
70:         raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Election not found")
71:     await db.delete(election)
72:     await db.commit()

6: from app.schemas import VoterCreate, LoginSchema

9: from app.utils.jwt_handler import create_access_token
10: 
11: router = APIRouter()
12: 
13: 
14: # REGISTER API
15: @router.post("/register")
16: def register(voter: VoterCreate, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
17: 
18:     existing = db.query(Voter).filter(Voter.email == voter.email).first()
19: 
20:     if existing:
21:         raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Email already exists")
22: 
23:     hashed = hash_password(voter.password)
24: 
25:     new_voter = Voter(
26:         full_name=voter.full_name,
27:         email=voter.email,
28:         password=hashed,
29:         cnic=voter.cnic,
30:         district=voter.district
31:     )
32: 
33:     db.add(new_voter)
34:     db.commit()
35: 
36:     return {"message": "User registered successfully"}
37: 
38: 
39: # LOGIN API
40: @router.post("/login")
41: def login(user: LoginSchema, db: Session = Depends(get_db)):
42: 
43:     voter = db.query(Voter).filter(Voter.email == user.email).first()
44: 
45:     if not voter:
46:         raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid email")
47: 
48:     if not verify_password(user.password, voter.password):
49:         raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Invalid password")
50: 
51:     token = create_access_token(data={"sub": voter.email})
52: 
53:     return {
54:         "access_token": token,
55:         "token_type": "bearer"
56:     }

1: import hashlib
2: import uuid
3: import logging
4: from datetime import datetime
5: from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession
6: from sqlalchemy.future import select
7: from sqlalchemy import desc
8: from app.models import BlockchainTransaction
9: 
10: logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
11: 
12: def _hash_block(block_index: int, previous_block_hash: str, receipt_hash: str, timestamp: datetime) -> str:
13:     payload = f"{block_index}|{previous_block_hash}|{receipt_hash}|{timestamp.isoformat()}"
14:     return hashlib.sha256(payload.encode('utf-8')).hexdigest()
15: 
16: async def _create_genesis_block(db: AsyncSession):
17:     # Genesis block has index 0, hardcoded previous hash, and a generic receipt_hash
18:     timestamp = datetime.utcnow()
19:     current_hash = _hash_block(0, "0"*64, "GENESIS", timestamp)
20:     
21:     genesis = BlockchainTransaction(
22:         receipt_id=None,
23:         block_index=0,
24:         previous_block_hash="0"*64,
25:         receipt_hash="GENESIS",
26:         current_block_hash=current_hash,
27:         timestamp=timestamp,
28:         status="Valid"
29:     )
30:     db.add(genesis)
31:     await db.commit()
32:     await db.refresh(genesis)
33:     return genesis
34: 
35: async def get_latest_block(db: AsyncSession):
36:     result = await db.execute(select(BlockchainTransaction).order_by(desc(BlockchainTransaction.block_index)).limit(1))
37:     latest = result.scalars().first()
38:     if not latest:
39:         latest = await _create_genesis_block(db)
40:     return latest
41: 
42: async def add_block(db: AsyncSession, receipt_id: uuid.UUID, receipt_hash: str):
43:     latest_block = await get_latest_block(db)
44:     
45:     new_index = latest_block.block_index + 1
46:     timestamp = datetime.utcnow()
47:     new_hash = _hash_block(new_index, latest_block.current_block_hash, receipt_hash, timestamp)
48:     
49:     new_block = BlockchainTransaction(
50:         receipt_id=receipt_id,
51:         block_index=new_index,
52:         previous_block_hash=latest_block.current_block_hash,
53:         receipt_hash=receipt_hash,
54:         current_block_hash=new_hash,
55:         timestamp=timestamp,
56:         status="Valid"
57:     )
58:     db.add(new_block)
59:     await db.commit()
60:     await db.refresh(new_block)
61:     return new_block
62: 
63: async def validate_chain(db: AsyncSession):
64:     result = await db.execute(select(BlockchainTransaction).order_by(BlockchainTransaction.block_index))
65:     blocks = result.scalars().all()
66:     
67:     if not blocks:
68:         return "VALID"
69:         
70:     for i in range(1, len(blocks)):
71:         prev_block = blocks[i-1]
72:         curr_block = blocks[i]
73:         
74:         # Check chain link
75:         if curr_block.previous_block_hash != prev_block.current_block_hash:
76:             return "INVALID"
77:             
78:         # Check current block hash calculation
79:         expected_hash = _hash_block(curr_block.block_index, curr_block.previous_block_hash, curr_block.receipt_hash, curr_block.timestamp)
80:         if expected_hash != curr_block.current_block_hash:
81:             return "INVALID"
82:             
83:     return "VALID"
84: 
85: # FYP Helper Methods
86: async def get_all_blocks(db: AsyncSession):
87:     result = await db.execute(select(BlockchainTransaction).order_by(BlockchainTransaction.block_index))
88:     return result.scalars().all()
89: 
90: async def get_block_by_index(db: AsyncSession, index: int):
91:     result = await db.execute(select(BlockchainTransaction).where(BlockchainTransaction.block_index == index))
92:     return result.scalars().first()
93: 
94: async def get_block_by_receipt(db: AsyncSession, receipt_id: uuid.UUID):
95:     result = await db.execute(select(BlockchainTransaction).where(BlockchainTransaction.receipt_id == receipt_id))
96:     return result.scalars().first()

16:     try:

28:             status='Generated'
29:         )
30:

32:         await db.commit()
33:         await db.refresh(receipt)
34:         
35:         # Layer 2: Generate Cryptographic Hash after commit

38:             receipt.cryptographic_hash = hash_val
39:             await db.commit()
40:             await db.refresh(receipt)
41:

43:             try:
44:                 new_block = await add_block(db, receipt.receipt_id, hash_val)
45:                 
46:                 # Layer 4: Simulated District Synchronization
47:                 # Rule 8: Sync failures never affect votes/receipts/hashes/blockchain
48:                 try:
49:                     await sync_block(db, new_block)
50:                 except Exception as sync_e:
51:                     logger.error(f"Failed to synchronize block to districts: {sync_e}")
52:                     
53:             except Exception as block_e:
54:                 logger.error(f"Failed to add blockchain block for receipt {receipt.receipt_id}: {block_e}")
55:                 
56:         except Exception as hash_e:
57:             # Rule 6: If hash generation fails, log it but never roll back or delete the receipt
58:             logger.error(f"Failed to generate hash for receipt {receipt.receipt_id}: {hash_e}")
59: 
60:         return receipt
61:     except Exception as e:
62:         # Log the error, but do NOT re-raise to ensure the vote is not rolled back

64:         return None

13: async def verify_voter_receipt(db: AsyncSession, receipt_code: str):
14:     timestamp = datetime.utcnow()
15:     result = {
16:         "verification_status": "NOT VERIFIED",
17:         "receipt_found": False,
18:         "hash_valid": False,
19:         "blockchain_valid": False,
20:         "district_sync_valid": False,
21:         "verification_timestamp": timestamp.isoformat()
22:     }
23:     
24:     # 1. Locate receipt_code in Votes (this is what the voter has)
25:     # Wait, the user might input the verification_code from VoteReceipt instead.
26:     # Let's check both Vote.receipt_code and VoteReceipt.verification_code.
27:     # The requirement said "Input - verification_code or - receipt_id. Analyze the current project and choose whichever already exists."
28:     # We will use Vote.receipt_code to find the Vote, then find the VoteReceipt.
29:     
30:     vote_result = await db.execute(select(Vote).where(Vote.receipt_code == receipt_code))
31:     vote = vote_result.scalars().first()
32:     
33:     receipt = None
34:     if vote:
35:         receipt_result = await db.execute(select(VoteReceipt).where(VoteReceipt.vote_id == vote.id))
36:         receipt = receipt_result.scalars().first()
37:     else:
38:         # Maybe they entered the Layer 1 verification_code directly
39:         receipt_result = await db.execute(select(VoteReceipt).where(VoteReceipt.verification_code == receipt_code))
40:         receipt = receipt_result.scalars().first()
41:         
42:     if not receipt:
43:         result["verification_status"] = "RECEIPT NOT FOUND"
44:         await _log_verification(db, None, receipt_code, timestamp, result["verification_status"])
45:         return result
46:         
47:     result["receipt_found"] = True
48:     
49:     # 2. Verify Hash (Layer 2)
50:     hash_status = verify_receipt_hash(receipt)
51:     if hash_status != "VALID":
52:         result["verification_status"] = "HASH INVALID"
53:         await _log_verification(db, receipt.receipt_id, receipt_code, timestamp, result["verification_status"])
54:         return result

113: def ensure_phase_one_schema(conn):
114: 
115:     if conn.dialect.name != "sqlite":
116:         return
117: 
118:     statements = [
119:         'ALTER TABLE voters ADD COLUMN email VARCHAR',
120:         'ALTER TABLE voters ADD COLUMN password VARCHAR',
121:         'ALTER TABLE voters ADD COLUMN district VARCHAR',
122:         'ALTER TABLE voters ADD COLUMN registration_hash VARCHAR',
123:         'ALTER TABLE candidates ADD COLUMN district VARCHAR',
124:         'ALTER TABLE votes ADD COLUMN vote_hash VARCHAR',
125:         'ALTER TABLE votes ADD COLUMN timestamp DATETIME',
126:         'ALTER TABLE audit_logs ADD COLUMN timestamp DATETIME',
127:     ]
128: 
129:     for statement in statements:
130:         try:
131:             conn.exec_driver_sql(statement)
132:         except Exception as exc:
133:             message = str(exc).lower()
134:             if "duplicate column name" not in message and "already exists" not in message:
135:                 raise

138: def create_admin_token():
139: 
140:     expire = datetime.utcnow() + timedelta(hours=ADMIN_TOKEN_EXPIRES_HOURS)
141: 
142:     payload = {
143:         "sub": ADMIN_USERNAME,
144:         "role": "admin",
145:         "exp": expire
146:     }
147: 
148:     return jwt.encode(
149:         payload,
150:         ADMIN_JWT_SECRET,
151:         algorithm=ADMIN_TOKEN_ALGORITHM
152:     )
153: 
154: 
155: def require_admin(
156: 
157:     credentials: HTTPAuthorizationCredentials = Depends(security)
158: 
159: ):
160: 
161:     if not ADMIN_USERNAME or not ADMIN_PASSWORD:
162: 
163:         raise HTTPException(
164:             status_code=status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
165:             detail="Admin credentials not configured"
166:         )
167: 
168:     if not credentials:
169: 
170:         raise HTTPException(
171:             status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
172:             detail="Not authenticated"
173:         )
174: 
175:     try:
176: 
177:         payload = jwt.decode(
178:             credentials.credentials,
179:             ADMIN_JWT_SECRET,
180:             algorithms=[ADMIN_TOKEN_ALGORITHM]
181:         )
182: 
183:     except JWTError:
184: 
185:         raise HTTPException(
186:             status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
187:             detail="Invalid token"
188:         )
189: 
190:     if payload.get("sub") != ADMIN_USERNAME or payload.get("role") != "admin":
191: 
192:         raise HTTPException(
193:             status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
194:             detail="Not authorized"
195:         )
196: 
197:     return payload

63: async def validate_chain(db: AsyncSession):
64:     result = await db.execute(select(BlockchainTransaction).order_by(BlockchainTransaction.block_index))
65:     blocks = result.scalars().all()
66:     
67:     if not blocks:
68:         return "VALID"
69:         
70:     for i in range(1, len(blocks)):
71:         prev_block = blocks[i-1]
72:         curr_block = blocks[i]
73:         
74:         # Check chain link
75:         if curr_block.previous_block_hash != prev_block.current_block_hash:
76:             return "INVALID"
77:             
78:         # Check current block hash calculation
79:         expected_hash = _hash_block(curr_block.block_index, curr_block.previous_block_hash, curr_block.receipt_hash, curr_block.timestamp)
80:         if expected_hash != curr_block.current_block_hash:
81:             return "INVALID"
82:             
83:     return "VALID"

1: import hashlib
2: from app.models import VoteReceipt
3: 
4: def generate_receipt_hash(receipt: VoteReceipt) -> str:
5:     """
6:     Deterministically generates a SHA-256 hash using vote metadata.
7:     """
8:     # Use deterministic existing fields that won't change
9:     # receipt_id is UUID, convert to string
10:     # timestamp is datetime, convert to ISO format string
11:     
12:     receipt_str = str(receipt.receipt_id)
13:     vote_str = str(receipt.vote_id)
14:     election_str = str(receipt.election_id) if receipt.election_id else "NONE"
15:     time_str = receipt.timestamp.isoformat() if receipt.timestamp else "NONE"
16:     polling_str = str(receipt.polling_station) if receipt.polling_station else "NONE"
17:     district_str = str(receipt.district) if receipt.district else "NONE"
18:     vc_str = str(receipt.verification_code)
19:     
20:     # Combine uniquely
21:     payload = f"{receipt_str}|{vote_str}|{election_str}|{time_str}|{polling_str}|{district_str}|{vc_str}"
22:     
23:     # Generate SHA-256
24:     return hashlib.sha256(payload.encode('utf-8')).hexdigest()
25: 
26: def verify_receipt_hash(receipt: VoteReceipt) -> str:

28:     Verifies if the receipt's stored hash matches its deterministic hash.
29:     Returns 'VALID' or 'INVALID'.
30:     """

1: PROJECT FLOW — hc-verify
2: Date: 2026-06-02
3: 
4: Overview
5: This document describes the end-to-end user and system flow implemented in the project so far, from landing to receipt.
6: 
7: User-facing flow (high level)
8: 1. Landing -> Home
9:    - User visits the app (frontend). They can view information and click to `Auth`.
10: 
11: 2. Auth (Login / Register)
12:    - Frontend page: `frontend/src/pages/AuthPage.jsx`
13:    - Behavior: user logs in or begins signup. CNIC removed from signup.
14:    - On successful login, frontend navigates to `Profile`.
15:    - Backend endpoints involved: `POST /auth/login`, `POST /auth/register` (reduced response; does not return voter_id).
16: 
17: 3. Profile (Edit & Save)
18:    - Frontend page: `frontend/src/pages/ProfilePage.jsx` (new)
19:    - User edits `full_name`, `email`, `district`, optionally password.
20:    - Save action: calls `PUT /auth/me` on backend.
21:    - On success, frontend redirects to `Register`.
22: 
23: 4. Register (Face verification / Liveness)
24:    - Frontend page: `frontend/src/pages/RegisterPage.jsx`
25:    - Liveness detection uses client-side MediaPipe implementation (`frontend/src/services/livenessDetector.js`).
26:    - Register requires the user to be authenticated (token present).
27:    - Flow: start liveness -> capture face image/embedding -> POST to `POST /register` (or `/verify-face` depending on implementation) -> backend performs embedding check and stores embedding plus generates `voter_id` when verification succeeds.
28:    - After successful registration/verification, frontend redirects to `Vote` and passes `voter_id` in navigation state.
29: 
30: 5. Vote (Ballot)
31:    - Frontend page: `frontend/src/pages/VotePage.jsx`
32:    - Behavior: accepts `voter_id` passed in navigation state or uses JWT Authorization header if present.
33:    - Casting a vote: frontend sends either Authorization header (JWT) or includes `voter_id` in request body to `POST /vote`.
34:    - Backend records vote (Vote table) and returns receipt or status.
35: 
36: 6. Verify / Receipt
37:    - Frontend shows confirmation/receipt page (e.g., `VerifyPage.jsx` exists for receipt checks).
38:    - Backend may support `GET /verify/{receipt_code}` to check vote proof.
39: 
40: Key backend actions and endpoints (representative)
41: - `POST /auth/register` — creates a user account (no voter_id returned here).
42: - `POST /auth/login` — returns JWT used for authenticated flows.
43: - `GET /auth/me` — returns current user profile.
44: - `PUT /auth/me` — update profile (used by `Profile` page).
45: - `POST /register` or `/verify-face` — face verification and voter registration; backend generates `voter_id` on success.
46: - `POST /vote` — cast vote using JWT or `voter_id` in body.
47: - `GET /verify/{receipt_code}` — optional receipt verification endpoint.
48: 
49: Data & state notes
50: - `voter_id` generation: occurs after successful face verification/registration.
51: - JWT: used for authenticated flows; when present, used for requests; when absent, `voter_id` can be supplied for post-verification actions.
52: - Face embeddings: stored in `voters.face_embedding` in DB once verification completes.
53: 
54: Frontend components & files (where to look)
55: - `frontend/src/pages/AuthPage.jsx` — login/register UI changes (CNIC removed).
56: - `frontend/src/pages/ProfilePage.jsx` — profile editor and redirect-to-register.
57: - `frontend/src/pages/RegisterPage.jsx` — liveness UI, register flow, redirect-to-vote with `voter_id`.
58: - `frontend/src/pages/VotePage.jsx` — consumes `voter_id` and casts the vote.
59: - `frontend/src/components/Navbar.jsx` — "Account" label/icon removed.
60: - `frontend/src/services/livenessDetector.js` — MediaPipe liveness detector used by register page.
61: 
62: Backend files (where to look)
63: - `backend/main.py` — API endpoints and routing (auth, register, vote, profile endpoints).
64: - `backend/app/schemas.py` — request/response schemas updated for profile and auth changes.
65: - `backend/app/models.py` — Voter, Candidate, Vote, AuditLog models.
66: 
67: Recent fixes and safety notes
68: - Fixed runtime SPA crash by removing/guarding undefined `speak()` calls that occurred during model load.
69: - Frontend build verified via Vite builds after each change.
70: - Security recommendations remain: move JWT secrets to environment, switch to bcrypt, add vote hashing/audit ledger, add tests and CI.
71: 
72: End-to-end sequence diagram (text)
73: 1. User -> Frontend: open app -> click "Auth"
74: 2. Frontend -> Backend: `POST /auth/login` (or register)
75: 3. Backend -> Frontend: JWT (on login)
76: 4. Frontend -> Backend: `GET /auth/me` (Profile load)
77: 5. User edits -> Frontend -> Backend: `PUT /auth/me` -> Backend saves
78: 6. Frontend: redirect to `Register`
79: 7. Frontend (MediaPipe) -> capture face -> Frontend -> Backend: `POST /register` (face image/embedding)
80: 8. Backend -> DB: save embedding, create `voter_id` -> Backend -> Frontend: success + `voter_id`
81: 9. Frontend: redirect to `Vote` (pass `voter_id` in navigation state)
82: 10. User selects candidate -> Frontend -> Backend: `POST /vote` (Authorization header if JWT, else body contains `voter_id`)
83: 11. Backend -> DB: record vote; return receipt
84: 12. Frontend: show confirmation/receipt
85: 
86: If you'd like, I can now:
87: - Commit these new files and changes.
88: - Generate a simple sequence diagram image (Mermaid) and save it in the repo.
89: 
90: End of PROJECT_FLOW.txt
91:

1: PROJECT CHANGES & FLOW
2: Date: 2026-06-02
3: 
4: 1) Purpose
5: - This file documents all changes made during the recent work on the hc-verify project and describes the final user flow implemented.
6: 
7: 2) High-level user flow
8: Landing Page -> Auth (Login/Register) -> Profile (Edit & Save) -> Register (Face verification) -> Vote (voter_id auto-filled) -> Verify/Receipt
9: 
10: Details:
11: - Users must authenticate (Account) before starting registration.
12: - After login, users land on a `Profile` page to edit/save details; saving redirects to the `Register` flow.
13: - `Register` performs face liveness/verification; on success the backend generates a `voter_id` and the frontend redirects the user to the `Vote` page, passing the `voter_id` in navigation state.
14: - `Vote` accepts either JWT-authenticated requests or direct voter_id submissions (for verified-but-not-tokenized flows); it auto-fills voter identity and allows casting a ballot.
15: 
16: 3) Summary of functional changes (what we did)
17: - Enforced auth-first flow: users visit `Auth` before `Register`.
18: - Removed "Account" label/icon from the Navbar to reduce pre-login UI noise.
19: - Removed CNIC from the auth signup form and stopped exposing a `voter_id` at auth-stage.
20: - Created a `Profile` page (frontend) and added `PUT /auth/me` (backend) so users can edit profile and then continue to registration.
21: - After profile save, the app redirects to the `Register` page.
22: - Registration (face verification) now returns/produces `voter_id`; the frontend redirects to `Vote` with that `voter_id` in state.
23: - `Vote` page updated to accept `voter_id` via navigation state and to send votes either using JWT (Authorization header) or including `voter_id` in the request body when no JWT is present.
24: - Fixed SPA crash during liveness/model load by removing/guarding undefined global `speak()` calls.
25: - Ensured frontend builds successfully after each change (Vite builds confirmed).
26: 
27: 4) Files changed (high-level list)
28: - backend/main.py: added/adjusted endpoints (`PUT /auth/me`), modified `/auth/register` behavior.
29: - backend/app/schemas.py: updated schemas for auth/register and profile update.
30: - frontend/src/pages/AuthPage.jsx: removed CNIC, adjusted post-login navigation to `/profile`.
31: - frontend/src/pages/ProfilePage.jsx: new page to load `/auth/me`, edit profile, save -> redirect to `/register`.
32: - frontend/src/pages/RegisterPage.jsx: require auth before starting liveness; after successful verification/register redirect to `/vote` with `voter_id`.
33: - frontend/src/pages/VotePage.jsx: accept `voter_id` in navigation state; if no JWT present, send `voter_id` in vote payload; avoid sending null Authorization header.
34: - frontend/src/components/Navbar.jsx: removed "Account" text/icon from visible navbar.
35: - frontend/src/services/livenessDetector.js: (no major changes to implementation) but pages using it were fixed to avoid undefined helpers.
36: 
37: 5) Key debugging notes
38: - Root cause of blank-screen during liveness/model load: runtime error from undefined `speak()` global helper referenced in multiple pages. Fix: remove or guard `speak()` calls (safe no-op) across pages.
39: - Some apply_patch attempts failed due to file index shifts; subsequent focused patches succeeded.
40: 
41: 6) Commands run (representative)
42: - Frontend builds (run in `frontend`):
43:   - `cd frontend`
44:   - `npm run build` (Vite build performed successfully multiple times during edits)
45: - Backend dev serve (example):
46:   - `uvicorn main:app --reload` (backend runs during development)
47: 
48: 7) Remaining recommendations (security & roadmap gaps)
49: - Move any hard-coded JWT secret to environment variables (`.env`).
50: - Consider switching password hashing to `bcrypt` (passlib) instead of `pbkdf2_sha256` for stronger defaults.
51: - Add deterministic vote hashing and an immutable ledger/audit (SHA256) to ensure vote immutability.
52: - Add admin endpoints for `district-results` and real-time updates (WebSocket) as future roadmap items.
53: - Add tests and CI, and consider Docker packaging for reproducible deployment.
54: 
55: 8) Quick file pointer (edit highlights)

59: - `frontend/src/pages/VotePage.jsx` - consumes `voter_id` from navigation state and supports non-JWT voting.

1: PROJECT DETAILED ANALYSIS
2: Date: 2026-06-02
3: 
4: Purpose
5: This file breaks the project into phases from start to end and explains the main files, functions, user actions, backend actions, and data transitions in order. It is designed so you can compare the current implementation against your own phase list.
6: 
7: Important note
8: A literal line-by-line explanation of every source file in the repository would be extremely large and hard to compare. Instead, this document gives a detailed phase-by-phase analysis and lists the important functions in the order they are used. That makes it easier to compare against your phases while still covering the real control flow.
9: 
10: PHASE 0: PROJECT FOUNDATION
11: Goal
12: - Set up the app structure, backend, frontend, and shared data model for voting, auth, face verification, and admin operations.
13:

15: - backend/main.py
16: - backend/app/models.py

28: 
29: What this phase establishes
30: - FastAPI backend serves all API routes.
31: - React frontend uses Vite and React Router.
32: - SQLite-backed models store voters, candidates, votes, and audit logs.
33: - JWT authentication supports voter and admin flows.

35:

1: """
2: security_middleware.py
3: 
4: Implements:
5: - Rate limiting (in-memory, per IP)
6: - Account lockout after 5 failed attempts
7: - Input validation (CNIC, phone)
8: - Audit logging helper
9: """
10: 
11: import time
12: import re
13: from collections import defaultdict
14: from datetime import datetime
15: from fastapi import HTTPException, Request, status
16: 
17: # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
18: # RATE LIMITER
19: # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
20: 
21: class RateLimiter:
22:     def __init__(self, max_calls: int, period: int):
23:         self.max_calls = max_calls
24:         self.period    = period          # seconds
25:         self._calls    = defaultdict(list)
26: 
27:     def check(self, key: str):
28:         now   = time.time()
29:         calls = [t for t in self._calls[key] if now - t < self.period]
30:         calls.append(now)
31:         self._calls[key] = calls
32: 
33:         if len(calls) > self.max_calls:
34:             raise HTTPException(
35:                 status_code=status.HTTP_429_TOO_MANY_REQUESTS,
36:                 detail=f"Too many requests. Try again in {self.period} seconds."
37:             )
38: 
39: # One instance per endpoint type
40: login_limiter   = RateLimiter(max_calls=5,  period=60)   # 5 per minute
41: vote_limiter    = RateLimiter(max_calls=3,  period=60)   # 3 per minute
42: register_limiter = RateLimiter(max_calls=5, period=60)   # 5 per minute
43: 
44: 
45: def get_client_ip(request: Request) -> str:
46:     forwarded = request.headers.get("X-Forwarded-For")
47:     if forwarded:
48:         return forwarded.split(",")[0].strip()
49:     return request.client.host if request.client else "unknown"
50: 
51: 
52: # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
53: # ACCOUNT LOCKOUT
54: # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
55: 
56: class AccountLockout:
57:     def __init__(self, max_attempts: int = 5, lockout_seconds: int = 300):
58:         self.max_attempts      = max_attempts
59:         self.lockout_seconds   = lockout_seconds
60:         self._attempts         = defaultdict(int)
61:         self._locked_until     = {}
62: 
63:     def record_failure(self, key: str):
64:         self._attempts[key] += 1
65:         if self._attempts[key] >= self.max_attempts:
66:             self._locked_until[key] = time.time() + self.lockout_seconds
67:             self._attempts[key]     = 0
68: 
69:     def record_success(self, key: str):
70:         self._attempts.pop(key, None)
71:         self._locked_until.pop(key, None)
72: 
73:     def check(self, key: str):
74:         locked_until = self._locked_until.get(key)
75:         if locked_until and time.time() < locked_until:
76:             remaining = int(locked_until - time.time())
77:             raise HTTPException(
78:                 status_code=status.HTTP_423_LOCKED,
79:                 detail=f"Account locked. Try again in {remaining} seconds."
80:             )
81: 
82: admin_lockout = AccountLockout(max_attempts=5, lockout_seconds=300)
83: 
84: 
85: # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
86: # INPUT VALIDATION
87: # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
88: 
89: def validate_cnic(cnic: str):
90:     """CNIC must be in format 00000-0000000-0"""
91:     clean = cnic.replace("-", "")
92:     if not re.fullmatch(r"\d{13}", clean):
93:         raise HTTPException(
94:             status_code=status.HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY,
95:             detail="Invalid CNIC format. Use 00000-0000000-0"
96:         )
97: 
98: def validate_phone(phone: str):
99:     """Phone must be 11 digits starting with 03"""
100:     clean = re.sub(r"\D", "", phone)
101:     if not re.fullmatch(r"03\d{9}", clean):
102:         raise HTTPException(
103:             status_code=status.HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY,
104:             detail="Invalid phone number. Use 03XXXXXXXXX format."
105:         )
106: 
107: def validate_name(name: str):
108:     if not name or len(name.strip()) < 3:
109:         raise HTTPException(
110:             status_code=status.HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY,
111:             detail="Full name must be at least 3 characters."
112:         )
113: 
114: def validate_registration(data):
115:     validate_name(data.full_name)
116:     validate_cnic(data.cnic)
117:     validate_phone(data.phone)
118: 
119: 
120: # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
121: # AUDIT LOG HELPER
122: # ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
123: 
124: from app.models import AuditLog
125: 
126: async def audit(db, action: str, details: str, severity: str = "info"):
127:     try:
128:         log = AuditLog(action=action, details=details, severity=severity, timestamp=datetime.utcnow())
129:         db.add(log)
130:         await db.commit()
131:     except Exception as e:
132:         print(f"[Audit] Failed to log: {e}")
133:

1: import axios from "axios";
2: 
3: const API = axios.create({
4:   baseURL: `http://${window.location.hostname}:8000`,
5: });
6: 
7: // ── Auto-attach admin JWT to every request ──────────────────────
8: API.interceptors.request.use((config) => {
9:   const token = localStorage.getItem("adminToken");
10:   if (token && !config.headers["Authorization"]) {
11:     config.headers["Authorization"] = `Bearer ${token}`;
12:   }
13:   return config;
14: });
15: 
16: // Handle 401 Unauthorized globally
17: API.interceptors.response.use(
18:   (response) => response,
19:   (error) => {
20:     if (error.response?.status === 401 && error.response?.data?.detail === "Invalid token") {
21:       localStorage.removeItem("adminToken");
22:       window.location.href = "/admin-login";
23:     }

1: # HV Verify: Secure E-Voting System Documentation
2: 
3: Welcome to the comprehensive system documentation for **HV Verify**, a secure, biometric-enabled e-voting application. This document outlines the project requirements, architecture, technology stack, project flows, core algorithms, and a complete log of all modifications made to stabilize, secure, and complete the system. 
4: 
5: Written in accessible, plain language, this document is designed for developers, system auditors, and project stakeholders alike.
6: 
7: ---
8: 
9: ## 1. Project Requirements & Goals
10: 
11: The primary goal of **HV Verify** is to provide a highly secure, immutable, and transparent electronic voting system that prevents fraud, ensures voter eligibility, and provides auditing capabilities at every step.
12: 
13: ### Core Security Principles (The 5-Layer Security Model)
14: 1. **Layer 1: Paper Trail (Receipts)**
15:    - Every cast vote must generate a unique, human-readable voting receipt code containing metadata such as the timestamp, polling station, and voter constituency.
16: 2. **Layer 2: Cryptographic Hashes**
17:    - The voting receipt's contents must be deterministically compiled into a unique cryptographic fingerprint (SHA-256 hash). Any change to the receipt data will invalidate the fingerprint.
18: 3. **Layer 3: Simulated Blockchain**
19:    - Verified vote receipts are chained together in an immutable ledger (blockchain). Each new block points to the cryptographic fingerprint of the previous block, preventing historical records from being modified.
20: 4. **Layer 4: District Synchronization**
21:    - To prevent centralized data tampering, every new blockchain block is replicated across four independent simulated regional servers (Districts A, B, C, and D).
22: 5. **Layer 5: Individual Voter Verification**
23:    - A voter can input their receipt code at any time to verify that their vote exists in the system, has an intact blockchain hash, and is fully synchronized across all districts.
24: 
25: ---
26: 
27: ## 2. Project Architecture & Tech Stack
28: 
29: The application is split into a modern decoupled client-server architecture:
30: 
31: ```
32: +------------------------+             HTTP / REST             +------------------------+
33: |   React Frontend       | <=================================> |    FastAPI Backend     |
34: |   (SPA / Tailwind)     |                                     |    (Uvicorn Engine)    |
35: +------------------------+                                     +------------------------+
36:             |                                                               |
37:             v                                                               v
38:   MediaPipe Liveness                                                 SQLAlchemy ORM
39:   (Client-side Camera)                                              (PostgreSQL/SQLite)
40: ```
41: 
42: ### Technology Stack
43: * **Frontend**:
44:   * **React** (built with **Vite**): Runs a fast, Single Page Application (SPA).
45:   * **Tailwind CSS**: Provides a responsive, clean administrative and voter interface.
46:   * **MediaPipe & Webcam API**: Evaluates facial movements directly in the browser to detect human liveness.
47: * **Backend**:
48:   * **FastAPI**: A high-performance Python web framework for serving APIs.
49:   * **SQLAlchemy**: An Object-Relational Mapper (ORM) used to model and query database tables.
50:   * **Uvicorn**: An ASGI web server engine powering the FastAPI application.
51: * **Database**:
52:   * **PostgreSQL / SQLite**: Stores persistent tables for users, voters, candidates, blockchain blocks, and synchronization logs.
53: 
54: ---
55: 
56: ## 3. End-to-End System Flows
57: 
58: The user journey is structured to ensure security checks are completed in a strict order before a ballot is cast:
59: 
60: ### Voter Workflow
61: 1. **Landing & Overview**: The voter lands on the website to review instructions and statistics.
62: 2. **Account Sign Up / Log In**: The voter registers an account with an email and password.
63: 3. **Profile Settings**: The voter inputs their personal details (full name, district, constituency) to establish their voting credentials.
64: 4. **Biometric Face Liveness Check**: The voter completes a liveness check using their webcam (blinking, turning left/right). Once successfully verified, the backend generates their official `voter_id`.
65: 5. **Ballot Casting**: The voter selects a candidate. The system automatically fetches their `voter_id` from the secure session, registers the vote, and generates a print-friendly receipt.
66: 6. **Receipt Verification**: The voter inputs their receipt code on the public verification page to confirm that all 5 layers of the security system validate their ballot.
67: 
68: ### Administrator Workflow
69: 1. **Secure Admin Portal**: Admins authenticate through a separate admin-only login screen.
70: 2. **Dashboard Overview**: Accesses statistics (turnout rates, voter counts).
71: 3. **Audit Log Inspection**: Review all major system activities (logins, cast votes, registration failures) in a tabular format or download them as an audit PDF.
72: 4. **Integrity Checks**: Runs recalculation checks across the entire database to immediately detect if any vote or voter registration record has been tampered with.
73: 5. **Election Security Inspection**: View the real-time state of the blockchain ledger and the regional synchronization logs.
74: 
75: ---
76: 
77: ## 4. Core Features, Algorithms, & Pseudocode
78: 
79: ### A. Biometric Liveness Detection (Frontend)
80: Before a user is registered, the system uses the client's webcam to check if they are a living person rather than a static photograph.
81: 
82: **Simple Explanation**: The browser camera tracks specific landmarks on the face (such as the eyelids and nose position) and calculates distances between them over time.
83: 
84: **Pseudocode**:
85: ```text
86: IF webcam_feed is active:
87:     Locate eye_landmark_points
88:     Calculate Eye_Aspect_Ratio (distance between top and bottom eyelid)
89:     
90:     IF Eye_Aspect_Ratio is less than blink_threshold:
91:         Record "Blink Detected"
92:         
93:     Locate nose_landmark_point
94:     IF nose_position moves significantly left or right:
95:         Record "Head Turn Detected"
96:         
97:     IF blink_detected AND head_turn_detected:
98:         Liveness = SUCCESS
99:         Submit captured face image to backend
100: ```
101: 
102: ---
103: 
104: ### B. Blockchain Block Generation (Backend)
105: When a vote is cast and a receipt hash is generated, the backend chains the receipt to the existing blocks.
106: 
107: **Simple Explanation**: We take the transaction index, the previous block's hash, the receipt fingerprint, and the timestamp. We merge them into a single text string and encrypt it using SHA-256.
108: 
109: **Pseudocode**:
110: ```text
111: FUNCTION generate_block(receipt_id, receipt_hash):
112:     latest_block = get_newest_block_from_database()
113:     new_index = latest_block.index + 1
114:     current_time = get_current_utc_time()
115:     
116:     # Merge block components with separator bars
117:     payload = new_index + "|" + latest_block.hash + "|" + receipt_hash + "|" + current_time
118:     
119:     # Encrypt the payload
120:     new_block_hash = sha256_encrypt(payload)
121:     
122:     Save new_block to database
123:     RETURN new_block
124: ```
125: 
126: ---
127: 
128: ### C. Timezone-Normalizing Integrity Verification
129: During verification, the backend must recalculate the cryptographic hashes of blocks and sync logs to verify they have not been altered. 
130: 
131: **Simple Explanation**: Because Python creates naive datetimes (no timezone info) which PostgreSQL automatically stores as timezone-aware timestamps (with offset indicators like `+00:00`), recalculating the hash directly on database values would fail. We shift the database timezone to the local system time and strip the offset before hashing to guarantee consistent matching.
132: 
133: **Pseudocode**:
134: ```text
135: FUNCTION verify_block_integrity(block):
136:     IF block has timezone information:
137:         # Convert timestamp to local time and drop the timezone suffix
138:         normalized_time = block.timestamp.convert_to_local_timezone().strip_timezone()
139:     ELSE:
140:         normalized_time = block.timestamp
141:         
142:     payload = block.index + "|" + block.previous_hash + "|" + block.receipt_hash + "|" + normalized_time
143:     recalculated_hash = sha256_encrypt(payload)
144:     
145:     IF recalculated_hash equals block.stored_hash:
146:         RETURN "VALID"
147:     ELSE:
148:         RETURN "INVALID"
149: ```
150: 
151: ---
152: 
153: ## 5. Complete Log of All Modifications
154: 
155: This log details the specific adjustments, bugs resolved, and enhancements made throughout the development and stabilization phases of the project:
156: 
157: ### 1. Speech Synthesis Crash Resolution
158: * **Problem**: When loading the Liveness Verification and Voting pages on some browsers, the application would crash and show a blank screen due to calls to an undefined global `speak()` helper.
159: * **Fix**: Replaced all undefined global `speak()` references in `RegisterPage.jsx`, `VotePage.jsx`, and `VerifyPage.jsx` with safe local no-op fallbacks (`const speak = () => {};`).
160: 
161: ### 2. User Sign Up and Profile Decoupling
162: * **Problem**: The registration process asked for a CNIC during initial sign-up and prematurely generated/exposed a `voter_id` before liveness verification took place.
163: * **Fix**:
164:   * Removed the CNIC field from the frontend sign-up form in `AuthPage.jsx`.
165:   * Updated the backend `POST /auth/register` schema to process registrations without a CNIC and omit `voter_id`.
166:   * Added a dedicated `ProfilePage.jsx` where users can edit/save details (`PUT /auth/me`) before being redirected to the webcam registration screen.
167: 
168: ### 3. Concurrency Protection in Blockchain Block Insertion
169: * **Problem**: Under concurrent voting loads, database race conditions caused attempts to insert duplicate `block_index` values, causing vote receipt completions to fail.
170: * **Fix**: Implemented a robust retry loop (up to 5 attempts) in the backend `add_block` function (`blockchain_service.py`) that captures integrity errors, rolls back the failed transaction, retrieves the updated index, and reapplies the operation.
171: 
172: ### 4. Database Schema Restoration
173: * **Problem**: An over-broad text replacement accidentally deleted the `receipt_id` column definition from the `DistrictSyncLog` and `BlockchainTransaction` SQLAlchemy models in `app/models/__init__.py`. This caused district sync tasks to fail with `'receipt_id' is an invalid keyword argument` errors.
174: * **Fix**: Restored `receipt_id = Column(Uuid, nullable=True)` to both models, syncing the backend code with the actual database schema.
175: 
176: ### 5. Timezone-Offset Hashing Normalization
177: * **Problem**: The voter verification checks repeatedly returned `BLOCKCHAIN INVALID` and `DISTRICT SYNC FAILED` for legitimate receipts. This occurred because timestamps generated by python (`utcnow()`) were stored by PostgreSQL with a local timezone offset (e.g., -7 hours). When loaded back from the DB, the altered string representation produced a completely different SHA-256 hash.
178: * **Fix**: Modified `_hash_block` (in `blockchain_service.py`) and `_hash_sync` (in `district_sync_service.py`) to convert database-aware datetimes back to system-local naive times before string formatting.
179: 
180: ### 6. Admin Security Endpoints Restoration
181: * **Problem**: The "Election Security" dashboard page would fail to load and throw toast errors because the `/admin/security/blockchain` and `/admin/security/sync-logs` endpoints were completely missing in `backend/main.py` due to a mismatch in a helper replacement script.
182: * **Fix**: Added both route endpoints directly to the end of `backend/main.py`.
183: 
184: ### 7. Frontend Security Dashboard Render Crash Fix
185: * **Problem**: The "Election Security" tab rendered a blank page. The page was storing raw wrapper API objects (`{success: true, blocks: [...]}`) instead of the nested array data directly, causing React to crash when evaluating `map()` functions.
186: * **Fix**: Updated the state setters in `AdminPage.jsx` to target the correct nested arrays (`blocksRes.data.blocks` and `logsRes.data.logs`).
187: 
188: ---

48:                 try:
49:                     await sync_block(db, new_block)
50:                 except Exception as sync_e:
51:                     logger.error(f"Failed to synchronize block to districts: {sync_e}")
52:                     
53:             except Exception as block_e:
54:                 logger.error(f"Failed to add blockchain block for receipt {receipt.receipt_id}: {block_e}")
55:                 raise block_e
56:                 
57:         except Exception as hash_e:
58:             # Rule 6: If hash generation fails, log it but never roll back or delete the receipt
59:             logger.error(f"Failed to generate hash for receipt {receipt.receipt_id}: {hash_e}")
60:             raise hash_e
61: 
62:         return receipt
63:     except Exception as e:
64:         # Log the error, but do NOT re-raise to ensure the vote is not rolled back
65:         logger.error(f"Failed to generate vote receipt for vote_id {vote_id}: {e}")
66:         return None

12: def _hash_block(block_index: int, previous_block_hash: str, receipt_hash: str, timestamp: datetime) -> str:
13:     # Handle timezone differences between creation (naive) and verification (aware from DB)
14:     t_str = timestamp.astimezone().replace(tzinfo=None).isoformat() if timestamp.tzinfo else timestamp.isoformat()
15:     payload = f"{block_index}|{previous_block_hash}|{receipt_hash}|{t_str}"
16:     return hashlib.sha256(payload.encode('utf-8')).hexdigest()
17: 
18: async def _create_genesis_block(db: AsyncSession):
19:     # Genesis block has index 0, hardcoded previous hash, and a generic receipt_hash

21:     current_hash = _hash_block(0, "0"*64, "GENESIS", timestamp)
22:     
23:     genesis = BlockchainTransaction(
24:         receipt_id=None,
25:         block_index=0,

27:         receipt_hash="GENESIS",
28:         current_block_hash=current_hash,
29:         timestamp=timestamp,
30:         status="Valid"
31:     )
32:     db.add(genesis)
33:     await db.commit()
34:     await db.refresh(genesis)
35:     return genesis

38:     result = await db.execute(select(BlockchainTransaction).order_by(desc(BlockchainTransaction.block_index)).limit(1))
39:     latest = result.scalars().first()
40:     if not latest:
41:         latest = await _create_genesis_block(db)
42:     return latest

63:                 status="Valid"

67:             await db.refresh(new_block)
68:             return new_block
69:             
70:         except sqlalchemy.exc.IntegrityError as e:
71:             await db.rollback()
72:             if attempt == max_retries - 1:
73:                 logger.error(f"Max retries reached in add_block: {e}")
74:                 raise e
75:             await asyncio.sleep(0.1) # short delay before retry
76:         except Exception as e:
77:             await db.rollback()
78:             logger.error(f"Unexpected error in add_block: {e}")
79:             raise e

177:     } catch (err) {

179:       try {
180:         const textArea = document.createElement("textarea");
181:         textArea.value = receipt.receipt_code;
182:         document.body.appendChild(textArea);
183:         textArea.select();
184:         document.execCommand('copy');
185:         document.body.removeChild(textArea);
186:       } catch (fallbackErr) {
187:         console.log("Both copy methods failed:", fallbackErr);
188:       }
189:     } finally {
190:       // Always proceed to verify regardless of copy success
191:       setCopyState({ loading: false, error: "", success: true });
192:       setTimeout(() => {
193:         navigate("/verify", { state: { receiptCode: receipt.receipt_code } });

195:     }

7: // ── Auto-attach admin JWT to every request ──────────────────────
8: API.interceptors.request.use((config) => {
9:   const token = localStorage.getItem("adminToken");
10:   if (token && !config.headers["Authorization"]) {
11:     config.headers["Authorization"] = `Bearer ${token}`;

13:   return config;
14: });

17: API.interceptors.response.use(
18:   (response) => response,
19:   (error) => {

21:       localStorage.removeItem("adminToken");
